| Sable Vey Rook and Fenroth Moonharp | Sable has delivered sealed songs Fenroth was paid not to sing. | Players may be asked to steal, protect, publish, or destroy a song. |

Relationship rules:

- Trust with one legend can modify the first meeting with another.
- Betraying one legend can damage linked reputations.
- Some links should produce cross-discipline recipes, such as rune-forged clockwork, slime-safe alchemy gear, oath-bound arrows, or bardic spell frameworks.
- Legendary relationships should generate rumors before they generate quests, so the world feels connected before the player touches it.

### Legendary NPC Event Simulation Layer V0.1

Legendary NPCs can trigger world events when their personal needs, faction pressure, regional danger, or player activity reaches a threshold.

Event trigger types:

- **Need Trigger:** The NPC lacks resources, proof, protection, ingredients, maps, witnesses, or skilled labor.
- **Threat Trigger:** A faction, monster, noble house, dungeon, cartel, or magical accident threatens the NPC's work.
- **Reputation Trigger:** A player becomes famous enough, infamous enough, or skilled enough to attract the NPC's attention.
- **Market Trigger:** A shortage, surplus, illegal trade, or auction manipulation affects the NPC's region.
- **Relic Trigger:** A legendary item, divine artifact, forbidden spell, or ancient map surfaces.
- **Server Trigger:** A major player event, raid clear, settlement fall, dungeon death, dragon taming, or political shift changes the world.

| Legend | Event Type | Trigger Example | Resulting World Event |
|---|---|---|---|
| Hilda Hammerwake | Relic and need. | A legendary blade breaks or memorial ingots disappear. | Forge crisis, dwarven tribunal, rare material hunt. |
| Sable Vey Rook | Relic and threat. | A sealed package is opened by the wrong person. | Rogue pursuit, spell leak, Dawnspire warning. |
| Ormak Wolfbanner | Threat and server. | A treaty caravan is attacked or a border town falls. | Escort war, peace summit, barbarian raid defense. |
| Mallow Crown-Gel | Market and reputation. | Slime cores spike in price or players over-harvest slimes. | Slime strike, wetland defense, monster-rights hearing. |
| Saelira Mosswhisper | Need and threat. | Monster migration enters farm territory. | Nonlethal hunt, habitat repair, tamer trial. |
| Lethian Reedmark | Threat and reputation. | Mixed-race villages report masked patrols. | River defense chain, purist exposure, evacuation event. |
| Fenroth Moonharp | Reputation and relic. | Proof of an old lie is found. | Festival performance, noble panic, public reputation wave. |
| Caerion Silverroot | Need and relic. | A spell framework starts mutating after being copied. | Academy lockdown, mage trial, spell rewrite event. |
| Yumi Longwhisker | Market and need. | Silk, cloth, pouches, or uniforms enter shortage. | Merchant audit, auction storm, trade-route defense. |
| Kadrim Gearwise | Threat and server. | Clockwork monsters begin copying player tactics. | Machine outbreak, capture raid, artificer ethics trial. |

Event scaling:

- **Solo Scale:** A single player can resolve a rumor, delivery, crafting request, rescue, or local proof-gathering task.
- **Party Scale:** A party handles escort missions, village defense, complex negotiations, dungeon-adjacent retrievals, or multi-step investigations.
- **Raid Scale:** A raid is required when the event involves a regional siege, boss monster, corrupted relic, machine outbreak, dragon-born dungeon floor, or faction army.
- **World Scale:** The server is notified when the event changes a legal status, opens a new market, exposes a kingdom-level lie, kills a dungeon, tames a dragon, or shifts a major faction.

### Legendary NPC Discovery, Rumor, And Proof Layer V0.1

Legendary NPCs should usually be discovered through the world before they are met directly. Players can hear about them through guild boards, tavern talk, merchant gossip, bard songs, wanted notices, academy records, map annotations, auction rumors, faction whispers, or NPC memory.

Discovery rules:

- **Rumor Stage:** The player hears a partial truth, exaggerated story, or location hint.
- **Lead Stage:** The player gains a usable clue, route, item, witness, or contact.
- **Proof Stage:** The player finds enough evidence to confirm the legend is real and currently relevant.
- **Contact Stage:** The legend appears, sends a message, allows a meeting, or becomes available on a special board.
- **False Rumors:** Not every rumor should be clean. Some should be wrong, outdated, exaggerated, or planted by enemies.

| Legend | Common Rumor Channels | Proof Object Or Witness | False Rumor Risk |
|---|---|---|---|
| Hilda Hammerwake | Smithing guild talk, dwarven forge songs, broken oath-weapons. | Memorial ingot stamp or a weapon bearing her forge grief-mark. | Fake apprentices sell counterfeit Hilda repairs. |
| Sable Vey Rook | Rogue whispers, sealed courier tags, Dawnspire-adjacent codes. | A black-route token that moves when moonlight touches it. | Decoy packages are used to expose careless thieves. |
| Ormak Wolfbanner | Caravan guard stories, Ifai war chants, treaty escorts. | A peace-banner thread tied to a broken arrow. | Warmongers spread stories that Ormak wants conquest. |
| Mallow Crown-Gel | Alchemy shortages, slime-rights graffiti, wetland songs. | A royal gel seal that remembers who touched it. | Slime harvesters claim Mallow is only a merchant trick. |
| Saelira Mosswhisper | Hunter warnings, animal migration changes, forest shrines. | A calm beast that leads players instead of attacking. | Poachers imitate her signs to lure tamers into traps. |
| Lethian Reedmark | Archers Guild gossip, river maps, mixed-village notices. | A riverbent arrow lodged behind impossible cover. | Purists plant false ranger marks to frame him. |
| Fenroth Moonharp | Banned ballads, festival rumors, noble hush payments. | A song verse that matches a hidden historical record. | Nobles commission fake songs to ruin his credibility. |
| Caerion Silverroot | Academy notices, spellbook marginalia, student injuries. | A corrected spell diagram written in silver-green ink. | Reckless mages forge his notes to sell unsafe spells. |
| Yumi Longwhisker | Merchant ledgers, cloth prices, auction house whispers. | A contract clause only Yumi's school teaches. | Cartels blame Yumi for shortages they created. |
| Kadrim Gearwise | Artificer warnings, machine wreckage, repair tags. | A cog-core with apology notes etched inside. | Competitors claim his machines are secretly possessed. |

Discovery should interact with the HUD and map:

- Confirmed leads can create map markers.
- Unconfirmed rumors can create soft markers with vague zones.
- Purchased maps can reveal route clues but should not instantly solve trust requirements.
- Party members can share confirmed proof, but false rumors should remain personal until verified.

### Legendary NPC Home, Route, And Availability Layer V0.1

Legendary NPCs should feel like they live in the world. They can have homes, workshops, routes, seasonal duties, event absences, and emergency states. They should not wait forever in one room unless their role demands it.

Availability rules:

- **Home Availability:** The NPC can be found at a known base when no major event is active.
- **Route Availability:** The NPC moves between places for work, trade, diplomacy, study, or secrecy.
- **Event Availability:** The NPC relocates when a crisis, raid, treaty, market collapse, or personal quest begins.
- **Hidden Availability:** The NPC cannot be found until the player has proof, reputation, skill, or a trusted introduction.
- **Server Time:** Availability updates on living server time. Sleeping cannot skip these states.

| Legend | Home Base | Travel Pattern | First Availability Gate | Emergency State |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Hilda Hammerwake | Memorial forge in Wortor. | Travels to dwarven tribunals and battlefield salvage sites. | Smithing progress, unusual ore, or memorial weapon lead. | Locks the forge and calls for defenders during forge raids. |
| Sable Vey Rook | No public home. Uses black-route safe rooms. | Moves between courier shrines, rooftops, tunnels, and hidden doors. | Rogue reputation, sealed package lead, or Dawnspire clue. | Vanishes and leaves only timed instructions. |
| Ormak Wolfbanner | Ifai peace camp on the plains. | Escorts caravans, inspects borders, attends treaty camps. | Caravan escort board or Ifai reputation. | Raises a defensive warband if peace talks are attacked. |
| Mallow Crown-Gel | Wandering wetland court. | Moves with cleaner slimes, water levels, and alchemy threats. | Slime rescue, slime diplomacy, or alchemy shortage. | Calls a slime strike or wetland defense. |
| Saelira Mosswhisper | Deepwood listening grove. | Follows migrations, wounded beasts, and poacher trails. | Nonlethal animal handling or forest reputation. | Evacuates creatures before settlement conflict. |
| Lethian Reedmark | Hidden riverwatch lodge. | Patrols river crossings, mixed villages, and ambush roads. | Archery progress or mixed-village distress lead. | Moves villagers through secret river routes. |
| Fenroth Moonharp | Festival circuit and banned archives. | Travels between courts, inns, hidden stages, and witness homes. | Bard reputation, forbidden verse, or noble hush-money trail. | Goes into hiding before a dangerous performance. |
| Caerion Silverroot | Aulend academy framework hall. | Visits libraries, mage trials, and damaged spell sites. | Wizard study, faulty spell diagram, or academy contact. | Locks down classrooms and summons trusted casters. |
| Yumi Longwhisker | Emea cloth exchange. | Moves between auctions, caravans, banks, and silk farms. | Merchant job progress, market anomaly, or contract dispute. | Freezes risky trade contracts and hires escorts. |
| Kadrim Gearwise | Moving artificer wagon-lab. | Follows machine accidents, spare-part trails, and repair requests. | Artificer interest, clockwork bounty, or captured machine core. | Converts the wagon-lab into a containment workshop. |

### Legendary NPC Guild Board And System Integration Layer V0.1

Legendary NPC quests should plug into existing systems instead of feeling separate from the world. Their needs can appear through guild boards, market notices, faction letters, private summons, auction alerts, class trainers, and NPC-generated requests.

Integration rules:

- **Guild Board Stage:** Early hints appear as ordinary jobs, rumors, escorts, repairs, audits, or investigations.
- **Named Request Stage:** Once the player has enough proof or reputation, the legend's name appears directly.
- **Private Summon Stage:** At high trust or high urgency, the legend contacts the player directly.
- **Party Sharing:** If the player is in a party, all party members can see quest steps, time limits, urgency, and reward split options.
- **Raid Escalation:** If too many related failures happen, the quest can escalate into a raid or regional event.
- **Economy Tie:** Legendary quests can change prices, resource demand, auction behavior, shortages, and merchant routes.

| Legend | Primary Board Or System | Secondary Systems | Generated Request Examples |
|---|---|---|---|
| Hilda Hammerwake | Crafting board and smithing guild. | Dwarven politics, relic repair, rare ore economy. | Repair memorial gear, recover stolen ingots, defend a forge delivery. |
| Sable Vey Rook | Thieves Guild dark contracts. | Dawnspire clues, courier routes, anti-scrying tools. | Deliver sealed parcel, identify decoy route, extract a trapped courier. |
| Ormak Wolfbanner | Knights Guild and caravan escort boards. | Barbarian training, treaty events, border reputation. | Escort peace delegates, prevent ambush, expose treaty sabotage. |
| Mallow Crown-Gel | Alchemy board and monster-rights petitions. | Slime taming, sanitation contracts, wetland markets. | Stop illegal slime farming, negotiate gel prices, protect cleaner slimes. |
| Saelira Mosswhisper | Archers Guild gathering and hunting board. | Tamer progression, forest ecology, nonlethal capture. | Calm displaced beasts, rescue companions, gather habitat supplies. |
| Lethian Reedmark | Archers Guild and village defense board. | Mixed-race diplomacy, river maps, purist faction tracking. | Mark ambush points, defend a ferry, expose false patrols. |
| Fenroth Moonharp | Bardic performance notices and rumor chains. | Reputation, court politics, hidden history. | Guard a witness, recover banned lyrics, choose how truth is revealed. |
| Caerion Silverroot | Mages Guild and academy notices. | Wizard books, spell framework safety, forbidden magic debates. | Correct spell diagrams, test dampening runes, contain a spell mutation. |
| Yumi Longwhisker | Merchants Guild and auction house. | Cloth supply, contract law, price forecasting. | Audit shipments, break a cartel, stabilize pouch and uniform supplies. |
| Kadrim Gearwise | Artificer bounties and repair requests. | Clockwork monsters, machine ethics, companion crafting. | Capture a machine alive, rebuild a control core, prove a machine was framed. |

Progression use:

- Completing these requests can raise class, job, guild, faction, and world popularity progress.
- Failure should not always end the chain. It can create harder prices, angrier factions, rival involvement, or emergency versions of the same story.
- Legendary requests should occasionally compete with normal NPC needs, forcing players to choose between fame, profit, morality, survival, and relationships.

### Legendary NPC Memory And State Tracking Layer V0.1

Legendary NPCs should remember players, parties, guilds, betrayals, favors, payments, failed promises, heroic saves, selfish choices, and public reputation. These memories should influence prices, quest access, dialogue, summons, training, and emergency trust.

Memory layers:

- **Personal Memory:** What the legend directly experienced with the player.
- **Party Memory:** What the legend remembers about the player's party or raid group.
- **Guild Memory:** What the legend hears through guild reports, contracts, boards, and faction letters.
- **Regional Memory:** What local NPCs believe happened, even if the truth is messier.
- **World Memory:** Server-level achievements, scandals, raids, dungeon deaths, dragon taming, public betrayals, and world announcements.

| State Field | Purpose | Example |
|---|---|---|
| Trust | Measures earned confidence. | Hilda allows the player to touch memorial steel. |
| Debt | Tracks favors owed by either side. | Sable owes the player one emergency extraction. |
| Suspicion | Tracks doubt, lies, missing proof, and questionable behavior. | Caerion delays teaching dangerous frameworks. |
| Respect | Tracks skill recognition even without friendship. | Ormak respects a player who held the line under fear. |
| Betrayal | Tracks broken oaths, theft, exploitation, and deliberate harm. | Mallow refuses trade after illegal slime harvesting. |
| Public Reputation | Tracks what the surrounding region thinks happened. | Fenroth's song makes a player's choice famous. |
| Relationship Role | Defines current social position. | Mentor, rival, ally, contractor, enemy, patron, debtor, protector. |
| Access Tier | Controls shops, training, quests, summons, and recipes. | Yumi unlocks auction timing data at Tier 2. |
| Emergency Priority | Determines whether the legend contacts the player during crises. | Kadrim calls trusted players during a machine outbreak. |

Memory rules:

- A legend can respect a player without liking them.
- A legend can like a player but still refuse dangerous training.
- Betrayal should create gameplay, not always permanent lockout.
- Public lies can damage reputation even when personal memory knows the truth.
- Party actions can affect every member, especially during raids, shared quests, and reward disputes.

### Legendary NPC Repeatable Request Generator Layer V0.1

After the main three-part quest chain begins, each featured legend can generate smaller requests based on need, inventory, region, faction pressure, player skill, and world events. These keep the legend useful after their first story arc.

Request rules:

- Requests should come from actual simulated needs whenever possible.
- Requests can refill NPC inventory, stabilize local markets, unlock training windows, or prevent event escalation.
- Requests should scale from solo to party to raid depending on danger.
- Legendary repeatables should feel richer than normal tasks, but still use familiar systems like gathering, escorting, crafting, investigation, delivery, defense, hunting, and negotiation.
